 2544 |     function resolveBlogActivity(intent, userId) {
 2545 |         var packageManager;
 2546 |         var resolved = null;
 2547 |         if (appContext === null) { return null; }
 2548 |         try { packageManager = appContext.getPackageManager(); }
 2549 |         catch (ignoredPackageManager) { packageManager = null; }
 2550 |         if (packageManager === null) { return null; }
 2551 |         try {
 2552 |             resolved = packageManager.resolveActivityAsUser(intent,
 2553 |                 PackageManager.MATCH_DEFAULT_ONLY, Number(userId));
 2554 |         } catch (ignoredAsUserResolve) {
 2555 |             try {
 2556 |                 resolved = packageManager.resolveActivity(intent,
 2557 |                     PackageManager.MATCH_DEFAULT_ONLY);
 2558 |             } catch (ignoredResolve) { resolved = null; }
 2559 |         }
 2560 |         return resolved;
 2561 |     }
